司」）及 其 附 屬 公 司（ 統 稱「本 集 團」或「我         its subsidiaries (together as the “Group”, “our Group”, “we”, “our”
們」）秉 持「誠 實 勤 道、創 新 求 進」的 經 營 理        or “us”) adhered to its corporate vision of “Honesty and Diligence;
念，堅 持「正 派 經 營」的 精 神，篤 行「健 康 操         Innovation and Excellence（ 誠 實 勤 道、創 新 求 進）”, as well
作」，穩健踏實不躁進，持續優化並精進成為                  as upholding the principle of “Honest Operation（ 正 派 經 營）”
一 個 具 備「品 格」、「品 牌」與「品 味」的 三 品         and “Healthy Practice（ 健康操作）”, for the purpose of steady

                             Management Discussion & Analysis

經濟環境狀況                               ECONOMIC ENVIRONMENT

2018年中國生產總值(GDP)同比增長6.6%，增           The GDP of the PRC increased by 6.6% in 2018 as compared to
長幅度較去年回落0.2個百分點，2018年首三              last year, which was lower than the growth rate of the previous year
季度同比增幅分別為6.8%、6.7%及6.5%，最低           by 0.2 percentage point. The year on year growth of the first three
為2018年 第 四 季 的6.4%，雖 季 度 增 長 速 度 滑   quarters of 2018 were 6.8%, 6.7% and 6.5% respectively, while
落，開高走低，但仍符合中國政府年度經濟預                 the rate of 6.4% recorded for the fourth quarter of 2018 was the
期增長目標約6.5%，國民經濟持續平穩發展，               lowest. Though the quarterly growth rate was trending down with
產業結構調整和轉型升級持續推進，發展質量                 the first quarter delivering the highest one and the fourth recording
提高。                                  the lowest, it was still about 6.5% which was the forecasted
                                     annual economic growth target of the PRC government. The
                                     national economy continued to develop steadily, and the industry
                                     also experienced structural adjustment and transformation, thus
                                     enabling the upgrading of the sector as well as the optimization of
                                     development quality.


國 內 消 費 力2018年 雖 仍 持 續 保 持 增 長，但 保   In 2018, the domestic consumption in the PRC maintained an
護主義所引發的國際貿易爭端，仍在互相角力                 upward trend. However, disputes on international trade arising from
與協商，構成不確定的總體經濟環境及財務風                 protectionism were yet to be settled through negotiations, thereby
險，帶來經營上不可避免的顧慮。                      clouding the general economic landscape with uncertainties and
                                     financial risks. Business operations were inevitably subject to
                                     negative impact of these concerns.


行 業 方 面，受2017年 原 物 料 上 升 影 響，2018    In respect of the food and beverages industry, given the soaring
年食品飲料行業順勢調整通路價格，而景氣於                 prices of raw materials seen in 2017, the industry took this
第三季開始轉下，市場需求轉弱，收益增長放                 opportunity to adjust the price quoted in sales channels. The
緩。近年來消費者流行趨勢愈發難以預測及掌                 market started to slow down in the third quarter of 2018 when
握，2018年方便麵走勢趨向兩極，朝更低端與               the market demand diminished, thus lowering the growth rate of
更高價，消費族群近一步分化，飲料則為消費                 our revenue. In recent years, it has been more difficult to predict
者重新青睞經典產品，而為開發新的契機，各                 and understand the preference and taste of customers. The
家廠商不遺餘力推出新產品、推廣新類別，以                 instant noodles market even went separate ways in 2018, which
期爭取消費者認同，亦為流行趨勢轉變做好應                 was characterised by the trend towards low-end and high-end
變計畫。本集團聚焦經營，重視品牌價值，一                 products. Customer groups were further differentiated accordingly.
切以提高消費滿意度為最高指導原則，不斷創                 With regard to beverages, classic products became the top

Management Discussion & Analysis

業務回顧                                        BUSINESS REVIEW

2018年本集團持續推展聚焦經營、品牌建設，                      In 2018, the Group proceeded with focused operations and brand
貫徹執行產品新鮮度管理，落實「健康操作」，                       building and committed to the management of product freshness
穩 健 經 營。本 集 團2018年 度 收 益 錄 得 人 民 幣          so as to uphold the principle of “Healthy Practice（ 健康操作）” and
21,772.2百萬元，較去年同期增長4.6%，主要                  stable operation. The Group recorded a revenue of RMB21,772.2
業務表現說明如下：                                   million in 2018, representing an increase of 4.6% as compared with
                                            the corresponding period last year. Information about the Group’s
                                            core businesses are as follows:


食品業務                                        Food Business

本集團在方便麵上不斷研究與創新，並隨著時                        The Group continues to research and innovate its instant noodles.
代進步，消費者生活型態的改變，開發自熱食                        With the advancement of the times and changes in consumer
品、冷鮮麵與休閒食品，以期滿足消費者於不                        lifestyles, we develop self-heating foods, frozen fresh noodles and
同場合和時機，皆能享受便利與美味的食品。                        snacks, so that consumers can enjoy convenience and delicacy in

食品安全                                  FOOD SAFETY

本集團秉持「三好一公道：品質好、信用好、服                 We uphold the philosophy of “Three Goods and One Fairness:
務好、價格公道」的理念，向消費者提供安全、                 Good Quality, Good Credit, Good Service and Fair Price（ 三
健康、美味的食品。為提升食品安全管理體系                  好 一 公 道：品 質 好、信 用 好、服 務 好、價 格 公 道 ）” and offer
層級，本集團設置食品安全委員會與食品安全                  safe, healthy and delicious food to consumers. To enhance
中心，致力於食品安全保障系統的佈建、食品                  the level of food safety management system, we have set up
安全政策的制定，深化食品安全風險評估、預                  Food Safety Committee and Food Safety Centre to implement
警及監控，保證食品安全；承擔本集團的品保                  food safety assurance system, formulate food safety policy,
體系管理，制定並落實品質政策，保證產品品                  improve assessment, alert and monitoring of food safety risks for
質。                                    guaranteeing food safety, undertake quality assurance system
                                      management of the Group, formulate and implement policy of
                                      product quality for guaranteeing product quality.

產能策略                                       PRODUCTION STRATEGIES

本集團致力聚焦經營，於產品研發創新、建立                       The Group spared no effort in our operation, research and
品牌價值以及建構銷售通路上，不斷努力，以                       development, innovation, brand building and sales expansion,
期能滿足消費者不同的需求，並使企業不斷成                       aiming to meet customer needs, develop and strengthen the
長茁壯，進而產生最大效益；為使資源聚焦運                       corporation constantly and maximise the revenue. In order to
用，產生效益極大化，本集團生產製造除擁有                       focus the use of resources, maximize the benefits, the Group did
自有產能外，長期以來並與專業飲料代工廠商                       not solely rely on its own production resources. It also outsourced
合作（ 包括外部獨立第三方及關連企業等專業                      its production to other professional beverages manufacturers
代工廠商），形成策略聯盟，使本集團在基本                       (including external independent third parties and related party
的產能之外，得以委外生產方式調節產能，使                       companies). A strategic alliance was formed under such long-term
產能有一定的彈性，以致本集團之資源得以充                       co-operation with the external manufacturers, enabling the Group
分運用在聚焦經營之項目上，發揮最大效益。                       to outsource production mode to adjust production capacity in
                                           addition to the basic production capacity, providing the Group with
                                           production flexibility. Thus, the Group was able to fully utilise its
                                           resources on core operation and optimise its efficiency.

財務管理                                       Financial Management

本集團堅持以審慎理財原則的理念為基礎，適                       The Group adhered to the principle of financial prudence. It
度因應市況調節銷售及市場推廣開支、適當資                       sought to control risk variables and moved forward prudently by
本支出以優化和擴展基礎設施，以應對存在的                       moderately adjusting its selling and marketing expenses according
風險變數，紮實前進。本集團的財務部門根據                       to market conditions, and made appropriate capital expenditures
董事會批准及執行董事指導的政策與程序制                        to optimise and expand its infrastructure. The Group’s finance
訂財務風險管理政策，並由內部稽核部和內審                       department formulated financial risk management policies based on
部門定期審查。本集團的財務政策旨在減低利                       the policies and procedures approved by the Board and guided by
率及匯率波動對本集團整體財務狀況的影響                        executive Directors. These policies were reviewed by the Group’s
以及把本集團的財務風險減至最低。                           internal audit department and the internal control department
                                           regularly. The Group’s financial policy aimed at reducing impact of
                                           interest rate and exchange rate fluctuations on the Group’s overall
                                           financial position, as well as minimising the Group’s financial risk
                                           exposure.


本 集 團 的 財 務 部 門 提 供 財 務 風 險（ 包 括 利 率       The Group’s finance department provided centralised financial risk

調、外部學習等，不斷提升綜合能力，為集團核                 robust team through mechanisms like comprehensive training,

獨立非執行董事認為，本公司訂立之方                The independent non-executive Directors consider that the
法及程序足以有效確保交易乃按正常                 methods and procedures established by the Company were

58      統一企業中國控股有限公司 Uni-President China Holdings Ltd.
        Annual Report 2018 年報
                                                         企業管治報告
                                         Corporate Governance Report

 統一企業中國控股有限公司（「本公司」連同                  Uni-President China Holdings Ltd. (the “Company”, together with
 其附屬公司，統稱「本集團」）致力於確保高水                 its subsidiaries, “Group”) is committed to ensure a high standard of
 平之企業管治常規及程序，並深明良好的公司                  corporate governance practices and procedures and appreciates
 管治對於提高投資者對本公司的信心具有重                   that good corporate governance is crucial to enhancing investors’
 要意義。本公司董事（「董事」）會（「董事會」）               confidence in the Company. In the opinion of the board (the “Board”)

                                        REMUNERATION

以客觀及有效地進行。審核委員會成員認為，                    ensure objectivity and the effectiveness of the audit process
本公司之外聘核數師羅兵咸永道會計師事務                     in accordance with the applicable standards. Members of the
所（「羅兵咸永道」）為獨立核數師，並建議董                   Audit Committee were of the view that the Company’s external
事會續聘羅兵咸永道為本公司之外聘核數師，                    auditors, PricewaterhouseCoopers (“PwC”), is independent and
惟須待股東於應屆股東週年大會上批准。於本                    have recommended the Board to reappoint PwC as the external
年度，向本集團所有外聘核數師就彼等之審核                    auditors of the Company subject to the shareholders’ approval
服務已付╱應付之薪酬為人民幣7.0百萬元。                   at the forthcoming AGM. During the Year, the remuneration paid/

                                        OF LISTED ISSUERS (THE “MODEL CODE”)

以規管董事之證券交易。經本公司作出特別查                    10 to the Listing Rules to regulate the Directors’ securities

因受聘於本公司而可能獲得內幕消息的高級                     Senior management who, because of their office in the Company,

風險管理及內部監控                               RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL
                                        CONTROL

董事會整體負責維持本集團穩健而有效之風                     The Board has overall responsibilities for maintaining a sound and
險管理及內部監控系統。該系統包含界定且賦                    effective risk management and internal control system of the Group.
予有限度授權的管理架構，旨在協助本集團識                    The system includes a defined management structure with limits of
別及管理重大風險，以達成本集團的整體業務                    authority, and is designed to help the Group to identify and manage
目標，釐定本集團的風險狀況，監督風險管理                    significant risks to achieve the Group’s overall business objectives,
框架結構，審核主要風險及緩解戰略，以及保                    determine the Group’s risk profile, oversee risk management
障風險管理的成效及遵守相關法律法規。                      framework structure, review key risks and mitigation strategies
                                        and to ensure risk management effectiveness and compliance with
                                        relevant laws and regulations.

                         Corporate Governance Report

集團利用自下而上及自上而下的方式，確保擁   A bottom-up and top-down approach is utilised to ensure a holistic
有一套完善的風險管理程序。自下而上的方式   risk management process. The bottom-up approach is supported
以核心業務單位識別風險及確定風險評級為基   by key business units to identify and prioritise risks while the top-
礎；而自上而下的方式則評估及檢討核心業務   down approach reviews and assesses if risks are comprehensively
單位於實現本集團的目標時是否已全面識別    identified, prioritised, and properly addressed by key business units
風險、為風險劃分級別及予以妥善處理。管理   to accomplish the Group’s objectives. The management team is
層團隊為持續評估風險定期監測新出現的風    regularly monitoring emerging risks for continuous risk assessment
險，建立以風險管理為基礎的內部審核計劃，   purpose and for building the risk-management based internal audit
並以現時的風險擔保為目的週期性評估風險    plan and periodically assess the adequacy and effectiveness of
管理及內部控制的適當性及有效性。此舉提高   risk management and internal controls for ongoing risk assurance
了本集團風險及控制框架的有效性。       purposes. This enhances the effectiveness of the Group’s risk and
                       control framework.

低風險、保障本集團資產、防止及偵查欺詐及   to reduce risks, safeguard the Group’s assets, prevent and detect

角度評估與檢討本集團風險管理及內部監控    auditors and with reference to a report submitted by the internal audit

本公司內部審計單位隸屬董事會，在董事會之   The internal audit unit of the Company, which is subordinated to
授權範圍內及審核委員會指導下，與外聘核數   the Board, formulates the internal audit plan of the Group based

本集團之業務目標。              provide reasonable, but not absolute, assurance against material
                       misstatement or loss, and to manage rather than to eliminate risks

1    一般資料                                   1    GENERAL INFORMATION

     統 一 企 業 中 國 控 股 有 限 公 司（「本 公                Uni-President China Holdings Ltd. (the “Company”) was
     司 」）於 2 0 0 7 年 7 月 4 日 根 據《開 曼 群           incorporated in the Cayman Islands on 4 July 2007 as an
     島 公 司 法》在 開 曼 群 島 註 冊 成 立 為 獲               exempted company with limited liability under the Companies
     豁 免 有 限 公 司。本 公 司 註 冊 辦 事 處 之               Law of the Cayman Islands. The address of the Company’s
     地址為P.O. Box 309, Ugland House,              registered office is P.O. Box 309, Ugland House, Grand
     Grand Cayman, KY1-1104, Cayman              Cayman, KY1-1104, Cayman Islands.
     Islands。


     本公司為投資控股公司，而其附屬公司                           The Company is an investment holding company and its
    （ 與本公司統稱「本集團」）主要在中華                          subsidiaries (together the “Group”) are principally engaged in
     人 民 共 和 國（「中 國」）從 事 製 造 及 銷 售               the manufacturing and sale of beverages and instant noodles
     飲 料 及 方 便 麵 業 務（「中 國 飲 料 及 方 便              in the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”) (the “PRC
     麵業務」）。                                      Beverages and Instant Noodles Businesses”).


     本公司已完成其全球首次公開發售，本                           The Company completed its global initial public offering
     公 司 股 份 於2007年12月17日 在 香 港 聯                and its shares were listed on the Main Board of The Stock
     合 交 易 所 有 限 公 司（「聯 交 所」）主 板 上               Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”) on
     市（「上市」）。                                    17 December 2007 (the “Listing”).


     除另有說明外，本綜合財務報表以人民                           These consolidated financial statements are presented in
     幣千元（「人民幣千元」）呈列。                             thousands of Renminbi (“RMB’000”), unless otherwise stated.


2    重要會計政策概要                               2    SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT
                                                 ACCOUNTING POLICIES

     本附註列出於編製該等綜合財務報表                            This note provides a list of the significant accounting policies

     年度。                                         all the years presented, unless otherwise stated.

       變更性質                                             Nature of change


       香港財務報告準則第16號於2016                                HKFRS 16 was issued in January 2016. It will result in
       年1月頒佈。其將令承租人於資產                                  almost all leases being recognised on the balance sheet
       負債表確認絕大部分租賃，此乃由                                  by lessees, as the distinction between operating and
       於經營與融資租賃之間的區分已                                   finance leases is removed. Under the new standard,
       被移除。根據新準則，須確認一項                                  an asset (the right to use the leased item) and a
       資產（ 使用租賃資產之權利）及支                                 financial liability to pay rentals are recognised. The only
       付租金的金融負債。唯一的例外情                                  exceptions are short-term and low-value leases.
       況為短期及低價值租賃。


       影響                                               Impact


       本集團已建立項目小組，根據香港                                  The Group has set up a project team which has
       財 務 報 告 準 則 第16號 的 新 租 賃 會                       reviewed all of the Group’s leasing arrangements over
       計規則審閱本集團於去年的所有                                   the last year in light of the new lease accounting rules
       租賃安排。該準則主要影響本集團                                  in HKFRS 16. The standard will affect primarily the
       經營租賃的會計政策。                                       accounting for the Group’s operating leases.


       於報告日期，本集團的不可撤銷經                                  As at the reporting date, the Group has non-cancellable
       營 租 賃 承 擔 為 人 民 幣66,550,000                      operating lease commitments of RMB66,550,000,
       元（ 見 附 註33）。於 該 等 承 擔 中，                         see note 33. Of these commitments, approximately
       約人民幣8,952,000元與短期租賃                              RMB8,952,000 relate to short-term leases which will be
       相關，並將於損益表中按直線法確                                  recognised on a straight-line basis as expense in the
       認為開支。                                            income statement.

    2.2 會計政策之變動                       2.2 Changes in accounting policies

      本附註解釋採納香港財務報告準                      This note explains the impact of the adoption of HKFRS
      則 第9號「金 融 工 具」及 香 港 財 務             9 Financial Instruments and HKFRS 15 Revenue from
      報 告 準 則 第15號「來 自 客 戶 合 約            Contracts with Customers on the Group’s financial
      之 收 益」對 本 集 團 財 務 報 表 的 影           statements and also discloses the new accounting
      響，並 同 時 披 露 於2018年1月1日              policies that have been applied from 1 January 2018,
      應用但與過往期間所應用者有所                      where they are different to those applied in prior
      不同的新會計政策。                           periods.


      (a)    對財務報表之影響                     (a)   Impact on the financial statements

      由於實體會計政策的變動，過往年                     As a result of the changes in the entity’s accounting
      度的財務報表須予重列。                         policies, prior year financial statements had to be
                                          restated.


      採納香港財務報告準則第9號一般                     HKFRS 9 was generally adopted without restating
      毋 須 對 比 較 資 料 進 行 重 列，惟 有           comparative information with the exception of certain
      關 對 沖 會 計 的 若 干 方 面 除 外。本           aspects of hedge accounting. The Group used modified
      集團採納香港財務報告準則第9號                     retrospective approach while adopting HKFRS 9. The
      時採用經修定追溯法。因此，重新                     reclassification and adjustments are therefore not
      分類及調整並無於2017年12月31                  reflected in the balance sheet as at 31 December 2017,
      日 的 資 產 負 債 表 中 反 映，惟 已 於           but are recognised in the opening balance sheet on 1
      2018年1月1日的期初資產負債表                   January 2018.
      確認。


      本集團已使用全面追溯法採納香                      The Group has adopted HKFRS 15 using the full
      港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第15號，並 已 對            retrospective approach and has restated comparatives
      2017年財政年度的比較數字進行                    for the 2017 financial year.
      重列。

      調整。未有受到影響的項目並無載                     for each individual line item. Line items that were not
      入下表。所作出的調整會於下文按                     affected by the changes have not been included. The
      準則作出更詳盡的解釋。                         adjustments are explained in more detail by standard

      香港財務報告準則第9號取代了香                       HKFRS 9 replaces the provisions of HKAS 39 that relate
      港 會 計 準 則 第39號 有 關 金 融 資 產            to the recognition, classification and measurement of
      和金融負債的確認、分類與計量、                       financial assets and financial liabilities, derecognition of
      金融工具終止確認、金融資產減值                       financial instruments, impairment of financial assets and
      及對沖會計處理的相關規定。                         hedge accounting.


      本集團選擇於其他全面收入（「其                       The Group elected to present in other comprehensive
      他全面收入」）中呈列先前所有分                       income (“OCI”) changes in the fair value of all its equity
      類為可供出售的股本投資的公平                        investments previously classified as AFS, because
      值變動，原因為該等投資為持作長                       these investments are held as long-term strategic
      期戰略投資，預計不會於中短期內                       investments that are not expected to be sold in the
      出售。因此，於2018年1月1日，公                    short to medium term. As a result, assets with fair
      平 值 人 民 幣222,093,000元 之 資             values of RMB222,093,000 were reclassified from AFS
      產由可供出售重新分類至按公平                        to financial assets at FVOCI and net impairment loss of
      值計入其他全面收入，而減值虧損                       RMB33,345,000 was reclassified from retained earnings
      淨 額 人 民 幣33,345,000元 則 從 保            to fair value reserve on 1 January 2018.
      留盈利重新分類至公平值儲備。


      自2018年1月1日起採納香港財務                     The adoption of HKFRS 9 Financial Instruments from
      報告準則第9號金融工具導致會計                       1 January 2018 resulted in changes in accounting
      政 策 出 現 變 動，以 及 對 財 務 報 表             policies and adjustments to the amounts recognised in
      內 確 認 的 金 額 作 出 調 整。新 會 計             the financial statements. The new accounting policies
      政 策 載 列 於 下 文 附 註2.11。根 據             are set out in Note 2.11 below. In accordance with the
      香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第9號(7.2.15)           transitional provisions in HKFRS 9 (7.2.15) and (7.2.26),
      及(7.2.26)的 過 渡 條 文，由 於 本 集            comparative figures have not been restated as the

      •      銷售貨品的應收貿易賬款；                              •     trade receivables for sales of goods; and
             及

             資產。


      新減值模型要求根據預期信貸虧                                   The new impairment model requires the recognition of
      損 而 非 如 香 港 會 計 準 則 第39號 般                       impairment provisions based on expected credit losses
      根據已產生的信貸虧損確認減值                                   rather than only incurred credit losses as is the case
      撥備。本集團需要根據香港財務報                                  under HKAS 39. The Group was required to revise its
      告準則第9號修訂其有關各項此等                                  impairment methodology under HKFRS 9 for each of
      類別資產的減值方法。                                       these classes of assets.


      雖然現金及現金等價物亦須遵守                                   While cash and cash equivalents were also subject
      香港財務報告準則第9號減值規定                                  to the impairment requirements of HKFRS 9, no
      的規限，但並無識別減值虧損。                                   impairment loss was identified.

      損，有關方法就所有應收貿易款項                                  expected loss allowance for all trade receivables.

      按攤銷成本計量之其他金融資產                                 Other financial assets at amortised cost include
      包括已收關聯方款項及其他應收                                 amounts due from related parties and other receivables,
      款項，倘有關款項的違約風險低，                                which are considered to be low credit risk when they
      且發行人有穩固能力應付近期的                                 have a low risk of default and the issuer has a strong
      合約現金流責任，其會被視為擁有                                capacity to meet its contractual cash flow obligations in
      低 信 貸 風 險，並 因 此 會 以12個 月                       the near term, and therefore the impairment provision
      預 期 信 貸 虧 損 釐 定 其 減 值 撥 備。                     is determined as 12 months expected credit losses.
      於 重 新 評 估 後，於2018年1月1日                         The loss allowance as at 1 January 2018 did not have
      之虧損撥備並無重大變動。                                   material change after reassessment.

      (i)      退款之會計處理                     (i)     Accounting for refunds

      本集團有責任為在標準質保期內                       The Group’s obligations to provide a refund for faulty
      的瑕疵產品作出退款。本集團於銷                      products are under the standard warranty terms.
      售時使用所累積的經驗估計有關                       Accumulated experience is used to estimate such
      退款。由於個別產品的體積大及價                      returns at the time of sale. Because of the large size
      值低，故所退回產品的金額並不重                      and low value of each individual product, the amount of
      大。已確認的累積收益不大可能出                      products returned were immaterial. It is highly probable
      現重大撥回。因此，概無確認退貨                      that a significant reversal in the cumulative revenue
      的退款責任。本集團於各報告日期                      recognised will not occur. Therefore, no refund liability
      重新評估上述假設及估計的退款                       for goods return was recognised. The validity of this
      金額是否有效。因此，應用香港財                      assumption and the estimated amount of returns are
      務 報 告 準 則 第15號 對 退 款 並 無 會           reassessed at each reporting date. As a result, no
      計影響。                                 accounting impact for refunds while applying HKFRS
                                           15.


      (ii)     客戶忠誠度計劃之會計處理                (ii)    Accounting for customer loyalty programme

      本集團並無推出任何可能會受到                       The Group does not introduce any customer loyalty
      香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第15號 影 響 的           programme which is likely to be affected by the HKFRS
      客戶忠誠度計劃。                             15.


      (iii)    向客戶付款之會計處理                  (iii)   Accounting for payment to customers

      應 用 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第15號 可           The application of HKFRS 15 may result in the
      能會導致應付客戶之代價被記錄                       consideration payable to a customer recorded as
      為 安 排 之 交 易 價 格 扣 減，從 而 令            a reduction of the arrangement’s transaction price,
      所 確 認 的 收 益 金 額 減 少（ 除 非 該           thereby reducing the amount of revenue recognised,
      付款就明確的貨品或服務向客戶                       unless the payment is for a distinct good or service
      收 取 ）。因 此，2017年 之 收 益 及              received from the customer. As a consequence,
      銷售及市場推廣開支減少人民幣                       revenue and selling and marketing expenses for 2017
      474,585,000元。於2018年，收 益              decreased by RMB474,585,000. For 2018, revenue
      及銷售及市場推廣開支減少人民                       and selling and marketing expenses decreased by
      幣500,855,000元。                       RMB500,855,000.

    2.3 附屬公司                                       2.3 Subsidiaries

      2.3.1 綜合賬目                                       2.3.1 Consolidation

      附屬公司指本集團具有控制權之實                                  A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity)
      體（ 包括結構實體）。本集團就參與                                over which the Group has control. The Group controls
      該實體之可變回報承受風險或擁有                                  an entity when the Group is exposed to, or has rights
      權利，並有能力通過對該實體之影                                  to, variable returns from its involvement with the entity
      響力影響有關回報時，即屬對該實                                  and has the ability to affect those returns through its
      體有控制權。附屬公司由控制權轉                                  power over the entity. Subsidiaries are consolidated
      移至本集團當日開始綜合入賬，並                                  from the date on which control is transferred to the
      由失去控制權當日開始不再綜合入                                  Group. They are deconsolidated from the date that
      賬。                                               control ceases.


      集團內公司間之交易以及交易所產                                  Intra-group transactions, balances and unrealised
      生之結餘及未變現利得均予以對                                   gains on transactions between group companies are
      銷。未變現虧損亦予對銷。附屬公                                  eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated. When

    2.7 物業、廠房及設備                              2.7 Property, plant and equipment

      項目直接應佔之開支。                                       expenditure that is directly attributable to the acquisition
                                                       of the items.

      在建工程（「在建工程」）指正在建                                 Construction-in-progress (“CIP”) represents buildings,
      造 或 即 將 安 裝 之 樓 宇、廠 房 及 機                        plant and machinery under construction or pending
      器，按成本減累積減值虧損（ 如有）                                installation and is stated at cost less accumulated
      列賬。成本包括建設及收購成本及                                  impairment losses, if any. Cost includes the costs of
      已資本化之借貸成本。在建工程直                                  construction and acquisition and capitalised borrowing
      至有關資產完成及可作擬定用途前                                  costs. No provision for depreciation is made on CIP until
      不計提折舊。當有關資產可供使用                                  such time as the relevant assets are completed and
      時，成本乃轉撥為物業、廠房及設                                  ready for intended use. When the assets concerned are
      備，並根據下文所載之政策提撥折                                  available for use, the costs are transferred to property,
      舊。                                               plant and equipment and depreciated in accordance
                                                       with the policy as stated below.

      後續成本僅會在與項目有關之未來                                  Subsequent costs are included in the asset’s
      經濟利益很可能會流向本集團，而                                  carrying amount or recognised as a separate asset,
      本集團又能可靠地計量該項目成本                                  as appropriate, only when it is probable that future

       本集團在每個報告期末評估是否有                    The Group assesses at the end of each reporting period

       明於因為首次確認資產後發生一宗                    asset or a group of financial assets is impaired and
       或多宗事件導致出現減值（「損失                    impairment losses are incurred only if there is objective
       事 件」），而 該 宗（ 或 該 等）損 失 事           evidence of impairment as a result of one or more

       出現減值及產生減值虧損。                       financial asset or group of financial assets that can be
                                          reliably estimated.


       減值證據可包括下列跡象：顯示多                    Evidence of impairment may include indications that the
       名或一組債務人面對重大財務困                     debtors or a group of debtors is experiencing significant
       難，拖欠利息或本金，彼等可能破                    financial difficulty, default or delinquency in interest or
       產或進行其他財務重組，且有可觀                    principal payments, the probability that they will enter
       察數據顯示估計未來現金流量的減                    bankruptcy or other financial reorganisation, and where
       幅可計量，例如與違約相關的欠款                    observable data indicate that there is a measurable
       或經濟狀況變動。                           decrease in the estimated future cash flows, such
                                          as changes in arrears or economic conditions that
                                          correlate with defaults.

       借貸初步按公平值並扣除產生的交                                  Borrowings are recognised initially at fair value, net of
       易成本後確認。借貸其後按攤銷成                                  transaction costs incurred. Borrowings are subsequently

       法於借貸期間內在損益表確認。                                   value is recognised in the income statement over the
                                                        period of the borrowings using the effective interest
                                                        method.


       設立貸款融資時支付的費用倘部分                                  Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are
       或全部融資將會很有可能提取，該                                  recognised as transaction costs of the loan to the extent
       費用可遞延確認為交易成本，直至                                  that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be
       貸款提取為止。如沒有證據證明部                                  drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred until the
       分或全部融資將會很有可能被提                                   draw-down occurs. To the extent there is no evidence
       取，則該項費用資本化作為流動資                                  that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be
       金服務的預付款，並按有關融資期                                  drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a prepayment for
       間攤銷。                                             liquidity services and amortised over the period of the
                                                        facility to which it relates.


       除非本集團有無條件權利於報告期                                  Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless the
       末 後 將 負 債 的 結 算 遞 延 最 少12個                       Group has an unconditional right to defer settlement of
       月，否則借貸分類為流動負債。                                   the liability for at least 12 months after the end of the

       時間模式更能反映租賃資產所產生                                 unless another systematic basis is more representative
       的使用利益減少，則採用該系統化                                 of the time pattern in which use benefit derived from the
       的時間模式為基準。因磋商及安排                                 leased asset is diminished. Initial direct costs incurred
       經營租賃而產生之首次直接成本加                                 in negotiating and arranging an operating lease are
       於租賃資產賬面值上，並按租賃年                                 added to the carrying amount of the leased asset and
       期以直線法確認。                                        recognised on a straight-line basis over the lease term.


    2.28 或然負債及或然資產                            2.28 Contingent liabilities and contingent assets

       或然負債乃一項因過往事件可能產                                 A contingent liability is a possible obligation that arises
       生的承擔，而該等過往事件的存在                                 from past events and whose existence will only be
       僅可由一項或多項並非由本集團全                                 confirmed by the occurrence or non-occurrence of one
       權控制的日後不明朗事件的存在與                                 or more uncertain future events not wholly within the
       否確定。或然負債亦可為一項因不                                 control of the Group. It can also be a present obligation
       大可能需要耗用經濟資源或承擔之                                 arising from past events that is not recognised because
       金額未能可靠地計算而未確認之過                                 it is not probable that outflow of economic resources
       往事件產生之現有承擔。                                     will be required or the amount of obligation cannot be
                                                       measured reliably.


       或然負債未予以確認，惟已於財務                                 A contingent liability is not recognised but is disclosed
       報表中披露。倘耗用經濟資源之可                                 in the financial statements. When a change in the
       能性出現變動致使有可能需耗用                                  probability of an outflow occurs so that outflow is
       經濟資源，則或然負債將確認為撥                                 probable, it will then be recognised as a provision.
       備。


       或然資產指因為已發生的事件而可                                 A contingent asset is a possible asset that arises from
       能產生的資產，此等資產只能根據                                 past events and whose existence will be confirmed only
       本集團不能完全控制的一項或多項                                 by the occurrence or non-occurrence of one or more
       不確定事件的出現與否方能確認。                                 uncertain events not wholly within the control of the
                                                       Group.


       或然資產未予確認，惟於可能出現                                 A contingent asset is not recognised but is disclosed in
       經濟利益流入時於財務報表附註中                                 the notes to the financial statements when an inflow of
       披露。若經濟利益流入可被實質確                                 economic benefits is probable. When inflow is virtually
       定時，則對該資產進行確認。                                   certain, an asset is recognised.

    3.2 資本風險管理                                3.2 Capital risk management

      本集團管理資本之目的為保障本集                                  The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to
      團繼續以持續經營方式為股東提供                                  safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going
      回報以及為其他權益持有人帶來利                                  concern in order to provide returns for shareholders
      益，並且維持最佳資本結構以減少                                  and benefits for other stakeholders and to maintain an
      資金成本。                                            optimal capital structure to reduce the cost of capital.


      為維持或調整資本結構，本集團可                                  In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the
      調整向股東支付股息之金額、發行                                  Group may adjust the amount of dividends paid to
      新股份或出售資產以減少債項。                                   shareholders, issue new shares or sell assets to reduce
                                                       debt.


      與行業其他公司一樣，本集團根據                                  Consistent with others in the industry, the Group
      資本負債比率監控其資本。資本負                                  monitors capital on the basis of the gearing ratio. This
      債比率乃按債項淨額除以總資本計                                  ratio is calculated as net debt divided by total capital.
      算。

  (c)   法定儲備                            (c)   Statutory reserves

        中國法律與法規要求在中國註冊                        The PRC laws and regulations require companies
        的公司，在對權益持有人作溢利分                       registered in the PRC to provide for certain statutory
        派前從各自的法定財務報表所申                        reserves, which are to be appropriated from the net
        報 的 溢 利 淨 額（ 在 抵 銷 了 以 往 年            profit as reported in their respective statutory financial
        度的累計虧損後）中提撥一定的法                       statements after offsetting accumulated losses from
        定儲備。所有法定儲備乃就特定目                       prior years, before profit distributions to equity holder.
        的而增設。                                 All statutory reserves are created for specific purposes.

        作為外商獨資企業和內資企業註                        PRC subsidiaries incorporated as wholly-foreign
        冊成立的中國附屬公司，在進行當                       owned enterprises and domestic companies are
        年度除稅後溢利分派時，須將法定                       required to appropriate 10% of statutory net profits to
        溢 利 淨 額 之10%撥 作 法 定 盈 餘 公             statutory surplus reserves, upon distribution of their
        積金，直至法定盈餘公積金不少於                       post-tax profits of the current year, until the statutory
        公司之註冊資本50%為止。此外，                      surplus reserve is not less than 50% of its registered
        此等公司可在各自董事會酌情決                        capital. In addition, at the discretion of the respective
        定下將部份除稅後溢利撥入職工                        boards of directors, these companies may allocate
        福利及花紅儲備及任意盈餘公積                        a portion of their post-tax profits to the staff welfare
        金。作為中外合資經營企業註冊成                       and bonus reserve and discretionary surplus reserve.
        立的中國附屬公司，在其各自董事                       PRC subsidiaries incorporated as sino-foreign equity
        會酌情決定下將部份法定除稅後                        joint venture companies may allocate a portion of their
        溢利撥入法定儲備。                             statutory post-tax profits to the statutory reserves at the
                                              discretion of the respective boards of directors.

        法定盈餘公積金只能用於彌補公                        The statutory surplus reserves shall only be used to

  本集團之最終控股公司為統一企業股份                               The ultimate holding company of the Group is 統一企業股份
  有 限 公 司（「統 一 企 業」），乃 於 台 灣 證                    有限公司 (Uni-President Enterprises Corporation*) (“UPE”), a
  券交易所有限公司上市之公司。本公司                               company listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange Corporation.
  董事認為統一企業之附屬公司及本集團                               The directors of the Company are of the view that the
  之合營企業及聯營公司被視作關聯方。                               subsidiaries of UPE, the joint ventures and associates of the
                                                  Group are regarded as related parties.

                                                         parties:
